Calendar TODO — third-party notices for the Windows distribution
================================================================

This file accompanies the Calendar TODO Windows application and its installers. It lists the
third-party components that are contained in the distributed artifacts, with their licenses.

Distribution scope
------------------
* 297 dependency entries cover the Windows normal-dependency closure and the
  packages emitted into the static frontend. Link-time elimination may omit some code; proc
  macros generate code rather than executing in the installed app. Their notices are retained.
* Components that are used only while building the project (Rust build scripts and the npm
  bundler toolchain, including Vite, Rolldown, SvelteKit build tooling, TypeScript and the
  Tauri CLI) are not contained in the distributed artifacts and are therefore not reproduced
  here. Their terms are recorded in the source repository notice file.
* The NSIS installer engine and its bundled plugins are contained in the NSIS installer.
* The WiX Toolset build tooling produces the MSI; its licensing notice is reproduced below.
* The Microsoft Edge WebView2 SDK loader is statically linked into calendar-todo.exe; its
  license text is reproduced below. The WebView2 Runtime itself is not bundled.

Notes on specific components
----------------------------
* Mozilla Public License 2.0 components (cssparser, cssparser-macros, dtoa-short, option-ext,
  selectors) are used unmodified as dependencies. Exact versioned source archives are available
  below at no additional charge under MPL 2.0; the license text is reproduced in this file.
  cssparser 0.36.0: https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/cssparser/0.36.0/download
  cssparser-macros 0.6.1: https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/cssparser-macros/0.6.1/download
  dtoa-short 0.3.5: https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/dtoa-short/0.3.5/download
  option-ext 0.2.0: https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/option-ext/0.2.0/download
  selectors 0.36.1: https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/selectors/0.36.1/download
* Unicode-3.0 licensed components (ICU4X crates, tinystr, writeable, yoke, zerovec, zerotrie,
  litemap, potential_utf, zerofrom, unicode-ident) retain their Unicode copyright and terms.
* AWS-LC/BoringSSL/OpenSSL-derived code inside aws-lc-sys retains its original notices; the
  complete combined notice file from that crate is reproduced below.
* SQLite is in the public domain; the bundled libsqlite3-sys crate notice is reproduced below.
* The NSIS plugins shipped inside the setup program (System.dll, nsDialogs.dll, StartMenu.dll,
  NSISdl.dll) come from the NSIS distribution and are covered by the NSIS notice below;
  nsis_tauri_utils.dll comes from github.com/tauri-apps/nsis-tauri-utils; its MIT text is
  retained separately below. The NSIS LZMA component retains CPL-1.0 with its linking
  exception; WiX components retain MS-RL. The application MIT license does not replace them.
  The accompanying CalendarTODO-installer-sources-0.1.0.zip contains the exact NSIS 3.11,
  WiX 3.14.1 and nsis-tauri-utils 0.5.3 source snapshots, their licenses, generated installer
  inputs with machine paths normalized, and a component correspondence record. Obtain it
  alongside the installer from the same distribution location, at no additional charge.
  Distribute that archive together with these installers. No differing terms for the covered
  components are offered by Calendar TODO. Any separate project provisions are offered by
  the project alone and not by upstream contributors.
  Upstream sources: https://sourceforge.net/projects/nsis/files/ and
  https://github.com/wixtoolset/wix3/tree/wix3141rtm

Microsoft Edge WebView2
------------------------
The application displays its interface using the Microsoft Edge WebView2 Runtime, which is a
Microsoft component installed and serviced separately from this application. This distribution
does not bundle the WebView2 Runtime or its bootstrapper. The v0.1.0 NSIS and MSI installers
skip Runtime installation: they do not download or execute a Microsoft Runtime installer.
The Runtime must already be installed for the application interface to work. If it is missing,
use Microsoft's official page and follow Microsoft's Runtime installation instructions:
https://developer.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-edge/webview2/
Review the terms presented by Microsoft. Calendar TODO neither supplies replacement Microsoft
license text nor asserts the user's acceptance. This notice does not grant rights to Microsoft software.

The WebView2 SDK loader (WebView2LoaderStatic.lib from the Microsoft.Web.WebView2 NuGet
package, version 1.0.3650.58, as redistributed by the webview2-com-sys 0.38.2 crate and
confirmed byte-identical to that package) is statically linked into calendar-todo.exe; the
executable has no WebView2Loader.dll dependency. Its BSD-3-Clause license is reproduced below.
The exact SDK package NOTICE.txt is retained below. Its package-wide references to
Antlr3.Runtime and StringTemplate4 do not establish that those assemblies are shipped here;
they are retained as upstream notice context without asserting their presence in the loader.

Microsoft Windows SDK and Visual C++ runtime
--------------------------------------------
The application is built with the Microsoft Windows SDK and MSVC toolchain. It links the Windows
API and the Universal C Runtime through operating-system components (api-ms-win-crt-* and the
Windows DLLs listed in its import table). No Microsoft redistributable runtime DLL is copied into
the installer. Microsoft Windows SDK redistributable file lists are published at
https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/legal/windows-sdk/redist and apply only to files actually
redistributed. Absence of a separate runtime DLL does not exclude statically linked support
code or by itself establish compliance with Microsoft redistribution conditions.

Project license
----------------------
Calendar TODO original contributions are licensed under MIT, Copyright (c) 2026 ke4989.
The complete MIT license, Microsoft component additional conditions and these third-party
notices are embedded in the application under Settings > About & Support > Open-source
Licenses & Third-party Notices, and are included in the installer's offline license page.
Public source and release materials also contain the complete texts. Microsoft component
conditions apply only within their stated scope. Other third-party components retain their
own terms; the project MIT license does not replace them.

recipient of ordinary skill to be able to understand it.

5. Termination
--------------

5.1. The rights granted under this License will terminate automatically
if You fail to comply with any of its terms. However, if You become
compliant, then the rights granted under this License from a particular
Contributor are reinstated (a) provisionally, unless and until such
Contributor explicitly and finally terminates Your grants, and (b) on an
ongoing basis, if such Contributor fails to notify You of the
non-compliance by some reasonable means prior to 60 days after You have
come back into compliance. Moreover, Your grants from a particular
Contributor are reinstated on an ongoing basis if such Contributor
notifies You of the non-compliance by some reasonable means, this is the
first time You have received notice of non-compliance with this License
from such Contributor, and You become compliant prior to 30 days after
Your receipt of the notice.

5.2. If You initiate litigation against any entity by asserting a patent
infringement claim (excluding declaratory judgment actions,
counter-claims, and cross-claims) alleging that a Contributor Version
directly or indirectly infringes any patent, then the rights granted to
You by any and all Contributors for the Covered Software under Section
2.1 of this License shall terminate.

5.3. In the event of termination under Sections 5.1 or 5.2 above, all
end user license agreements (excluding distributors and resellers) which
have been validly granted by You or Your distributors under this License
prior to termination shall survive termination.

Additional source and artwork attributions
==========================================

Inline SVG line icons
---------------------
Some inline line-icon geometry corresponds to Lucide / Feather, including the
chevron-down path and parts of the reset-order refresh icon. Geometry has local
size, stroke and path adaptations; the exact originally copied revision is not
recoverable from the project history. Lucide 0.468.0 and Feather 4.29.2 are
reference snapshots for the reproduced license texts, not asserted source
versions for every glyph. Generic plus, cross, check and window-control shapes
do not establish copying from a particular icon library. No icon package is
added to the dependency lockfiles.

https://github.com/lucide-icons/lucide/tree/0.468.0
https://github.com/feathericons/feather/tree/v4.29.2

and runtime terms are in the dependency inventory. This software copyright

files and the tray icon previously depicted the Tauri mark. They have been
replaced by the project owner's selected Calendar TODO artwork. The Tauri
software attribution remains; it is not a grant of trademark rights.

Microsoft WebView2 Runtime: separate component
-----------------------------------------------
Official Evergreen terms are served by Microsoft's download page through:
https://developer.microsoft.com/microsoft-edge/api/eula/webview2?locale=en-us
The separate consumer terms are served with &consumer=true. These are Runtime
terms, separate from the SDK loader's BSD license. The developer terms include
distribution conditions and required SmartScreen end-user notices. Attaching
third-party notices does not establish acceptance of those terms. For v0.1.0 both Windows
installers use skip mode and do not download or install the Runtime. Missing-Runtime users
must follow Microsoft's official installation process and review the terms it presents.
This change does not settle separate MSVC/Windows SDK redistribution conditions.

Microsoft Defender SmartScreen is enabled by default in the WebView2 Runtime
and can collect and send end-user information to Microsoft as described at:
https://aka.ms/privacy
https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-edge/privacy-whitepaper#smartscreen
The Runtime is serviced separately and can automatically download and install
its own updates, independently of Calendar TODO's manual application updates.
